## 4. Packs, the catalog, and selection — semantics decided, source blocked

### 4.1 The block, first

**The selection engine's context-vector source is BLOCKED on two independent axes.** The **LLM-emitted** vector is blocked by **ADR-0079, status `proposed`** — the only non-accepted record in the 89 other than two full supersessions. The **derived** vector, which AC-071's gate never reached because that gate keys on `source = "llm"` alone, is blocked by **ADR-0089, accepted 2026-09-09**, on measurement that postdates the brief. Nothing in §4 resolves either, and nothing later in this document may. The two are not the same question and may not be collapsed: F39 measures a derived vector and narrows ADR-0079's options without deciding among them.

| What P1 measured | Reading | Tag |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Modal `R(stage, ctx)` identical across ten draws | **5/20, 3/20, 2/20 and 2/20** items against an **80%** pre-registered line — the kill fires **4 of 4** | [measured, F36; 400 dispatches, 0 void, $21.37, one corpus pin] |
| The prescribed anchor-rewrite repair | Did not work | [measured, F36] |
| `external_dep` | Collapsed to a constant — **200/200 false**; an emitted value carrying no information | [measured, F36] |
| Three axes with **byte-identical** anchors | Drifted up to **−0.168** — larger than the effect the `alpha < 0.6` rule exists to detect, so **that rule is not a decidable predicate on this corpus** | [measured, F36] |
| `unknown` rates | novelty **46.0%**, size **39.5%**, blast_radius **31.0%**, criticality **27.0%** | [measured, F36, n=200 draws] |
| Arm D precision / recall | **SCORED 2026-09-09**, against 140 truth cells at pin `47faec9f` under METHOD-P amendment 9. **No facet clears §1.4's 0.95 bar**, and the sub-0.95 clause fires on five: `ui` **1.00** [0.65, 1.00] recall 0.78 (tp 7, fp 0, fn 2, tn 6, excl 4) — the point clears, **the interval does not**; `toolchain` 0.73 [0.48, 0.89] recall 0.79 (tp 11, fp 4, fn 3); `change` 0.72 [0.54, 0.85] recall 0.84 (tp 21, fp 8, fn 4); `binary_change` 0.60 [0.23, 0.88] recall 1.00 (tp 3, fp 2, fn 0, tn 15); `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66] (tp 0, fp 2, tn 13, excl 4); **`auth` 0.00 [0.00, 0.26]** (tp 0, **fp 11**, tn 4, excl 4); `data_migration` **NOT MEASURABLE** — 0 positives on 20 items across all three sources, precision undefined, a perfect method scores identically, **the criterion has no denominator** | [measured, F39; 140 cells, **$0** model cost, **PANEL-ADJUDICATED**, never human-panel] |
| The 0.95 bar itself, at this n | **NOT REACHABLE for any facet however good.** `ui` is 7/7 and its lower bound is 0.65; roughly **60** positives are needed for a lower bound above 0.95. A 20-item corpus cannot pass this criterion — the same class of defect as P1's alpha rule (F36) and P7's criterion 1 (F37): a pre-registered bar the registered sample size cannot decide. This is a **result about the pre-registration**, carried in F39's Limits; it is not an amendment to METHOD-P | [measured, F39, Limits 1] |
| Why `auth` scores zero | Not a threshold miss and not a tuning parameter. The rule is an **unanchored, case-insensitive regex over paths and diff lines** and it marks `auth = true` on **14 of 20 items**. `token` fired 44 times, **36 inside a design token**; `auth` 43 times, **40 inside *authored* / *author* / *authors* / *authoring* / *report-md-unauthored* / *only-the-author-can-advance***; `oauth` 8 times, **all 8 inside *coauthor*** (`oauth` sits inside `c-oauth-or`); `session` 8, six a *work* session; `role` 2, both an ARIA attribute. **The failure is correlated, not random:** the colliding vocabulary is the vocabulary of the instrument under test | [measured, F39 §2] |
| Absence of evidence, emitted as a confident boolean | **4 of the 20 items changed no code whatsoever** — every changed file is `.bklg/` bookkeeping or telemetry. The panel returned `unknown` on **all 16** of their semantic-facet cells; the script emitted a confident boolean on every one. That is the `excl = 4` column. `unknown` is **excluded from scoring and never counted against the script**; reporting those 16 cells as agreement would have raised every semantic precision figure | [measured, F39 §4, Limit 6] |
| How the truth set was built | 140 cells: **60** deterministic oracle (git metadata, independently implemented), **39** unanimous panel, **9** unanimous panel where the adversarial flag was mechanically moot, **32 human adjudication** — Ryan, blind, the panel's and the script's positions revealed only after he answered. Ryan's verdicts: **8 true, 24 false, 0 unknown, and 15 of 32 differ from the script**. The panel labelled 80 semantic cells: 39 accepted unanimous (23 decided, 16 honest `unknown`), 41 flagged | [measured, F39 §5] |

The composition mechanism is the point, not any single axis: per-facet alpha looks healthy on 4 of 8 axes while `R` almost never repeats, because four axes each flipping on a minority of draws compose into an unstable required set, and `unknown` is treated as `high` by position, so an axis oscillating `unknown ↔ low` moves `R` the full band width [measured mechanism, F36 / brief C.9].

Three external results corroborate the instability and rule out the cheap repair:

| Source | What it establishes | Tag |
| --- | --- | --- |
| *Same Input, Different Scores*, arXiv 2603.04417 (Feb 2026) — GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Claude-Haiku-4.5, Claude-Sonnet-4.5 | "Despite expectations of stability at temperature=0, substantial variability persisted across models"; temperature=0 had limited or inconsistent effect for the Anthropic models | [preprint] |
| *Introducing Background Temperature*, arXiv 2604.22411 (Apr 2026) | Measures **Claude-Sonnet-4 at T_bg = 0** — perfectly deterministic in their harness — which places P1's instability on the **schema/prompt** side, not the decoding side | [preprint] |
| arXiv 2511.01176 (code-clone classification) | Instability **correlates with proximity to the decision boundary**; far-from-boundary inputs are typically stable | [preprint] |

**A temperature spike is explicitly not the answer** (research pass §4 rec. 21, fetched 2026-09-08). The one cheap diagnostic available against existing P1 data is arXiv 2511.01176's boundary-distance bucketing of the draws already recorded — it is carried in the spike register as **P1-BOUND**, a $0 sub-arm of P1-D (§10.4, §10.5), not here.

**What the block forbids, at full strength.** Phase 1 may not author a `require` row keyed on `criticality`, `novelty`, `size`, `blast_radius`, `market_facing`, `external_dep` or `has_repro` sourced from an LLM classification dispatch. `kind` is out of scope of the block: it is human input (ADR-0030 rule 4). Three criteria carry halves of this block that the prose above argues from and that were previously pinned only through AC-434's display scope: **AC-069** and **AC-079** fix the band rule itself — `unknown` is treated as `high` **by position**, so an axis oscillating `unknown ↔ low` moves `R` the full band width, which is the composition mechanism, not a display artefact; **AC-070** refuses a `require` row fed by a facet whose **measured distribution is degenerate** — F36's `external_dep` at 200/200 false is the named instance, and §4.5 pins the *registry* half of that through AC-072/AC-108 while AC-070 pins the *distribution* half; and **AC-074** is the mechanism §4.9's operator-load hedge and §10.2's "every ordinal axis is `source = human`" prohibition actually rest on — **a human override is stored as a decision-memory row and replayed on identical input**, so the human-sourced regime is not a per-run re-ask. **AC-073** keeps booleans and ordinals in separate registries so a boolean cannot be ranked. **The panel clause is discharged; the block is not.** F39 scored arm D on 2026-09-09, which satisfies **AC-071's fourth clause and no other** — clauses 2, 3 and 5 were already met by arm L's own record, 20 items × 10 draws at a pin, and that record fails clause 1: modal required-set identity **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against the 0.80 line [measured, F36]. So **every ordinal axis stays `source = "human"`** — supplied or confirmed at the triage gate — and the engine refuses an LLM-emitted value for it. That prohibition is not a prose hedge: it is machine-checked by **AC-071**, whose `redkiln lint --facet-stability` gate refuses pack load while any facet declares `source = "llm"` without a scored P1 record (identity ≥ 0.80, n ≥ 20 items, **≥ 10 draws per item** — AC-071's executable precondition reads `rec.draws_per_item >= 10`, and an earlier revision of this document wrote 5 — arm-D panel scored, corpus pinned).

**The parallel clause that formula was silent on, and that F39 forces.** "Every ordinal axis is `source = "human"`" says nothing whatever about `source = "derived"`, and that silence is exactly the gap: AC-071 keys on `source = "llm"`, so the semantic facets sat **outside the block entirely and are the ones measurably wrong**. Under **ADR-0089**, accepted 2026-09-09: **no facet may declare `source = "derived"` without a named `deriver` and a scored precision record for that deriver**, and a facet whose only computation is a lexical scan of free text does not satisfy PC8 rule 2 (ADR-0030) for `require`-row purposes — rule 2's prohibition is unchanged in force and strengthened in content. The repo-wide `auth` boolean is **withdrawn** and replaced by a **set-valued `surfaces` facet** over a checked-in `surfaces.toml` path-glob map, a fact that is the intersection of the changeset with a declared boundary rather than a lexical hit; set-valued is precedented by `toolchain` and `change`, though it is a **third registry shape** the loader must recognise explicitly rather than by omission. Provenance — **DECLARED > DERIVED > PROPOSED > ASKED** — is carried on the value so a `require` row may demand a minimum, and each activity's `applicable_if` owns its own evidence question (ADR-0024). `surfaces` is **unscored until S-FACET runs**: it may be authored and linted, and it may not key a `require` row on ADR-0089's strength alone. And **boolean facets become tri-valued — absence of evidence is `null`, never `false` — routed to AC-079's existing `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`**, the route the booleans could not reach because their declared domain is `[true, false]`. ADR-0067's `recent_deploy` already emits `null` under rule `R-recent-deploy-feed-absent`, so the pattern is **generalised, not invented**; abstention is reported separately from error, and a `null` cell is never counted as a false negative. **ADR-0089 does not touch ADR-0079** and does not decide source asymmetry.

**AC-076** carries the panel itself as a human gate node, explicitly not machine-decidable and with no threshold stated. Its warrant has changed and the criterion has not: arm D is no longer uncomputed, but METHOD-P §1.4's precision line is **not reachable at n = 20** (F39, Limit 1), so there is still no pre-registered precision/recall threshold this document can cite as machine-decidable.

**Three adjacent unknowns, named rather than glossed.** **P2 is entirely unrun** — its three criteria are unanswered, its labels unwritten, and the engine phase refuses until they are timestamped [documented, brief §B/§E]; it gates the `stale_policy` setting of step 3, so **AC-068** is marked PROVISIONAL and SM-021 records that P2's own precision/recall lines are **not citable from the brief**. And **`ChangeSummary`** is one of the five toolchain-keyed artifact kinds §4.3 expands per toolchain, but its **type design is blocked on S-BIN, registered and unrun** — so no criterion in this area constrains its content (packs-catalog gap 3). And **source asymmetry is a separate, untaken decision, registered as an arm of S-FACET and decided nowhere in this document**: `commits` beats `docs` on every facet F39 could measure — `toolchain` **1.00** [0.70, 1.00] against **0.50** [0.22, 0.78], `change` **0.80** [0.61, 0.91] against **0.50** [0.24, 0.76], `ui` 1.00 at recall 0.67 against 1.00 at recall 0.22, and `binary_change` **0.60 at recall 1.00 against no positives at all** from `docs` (recall 0.00, 3 missed), because a card names a path but carries no binary marker, so the facet is unreachable from prose by construction — and **`union` is worse than `commits` alone** on `toolchain` (0.73) and on `change` (0.72), because unioning a precise source with an imprecise one imports the imprecision [measured, F39 §3]. Which source the engine trusts is independent of the regex question and is not settled here.

**What is *not* blocked.** The engine's semantics are decided and buildable now: purity, the seven steps, rank, tie-is-a-validation-error, unsatisfiable-is-a-typed-gate, deviation-never-edits-`R`, replay, and the whole lint surface. Everything from §4.2 to §4.7 is written for a **given** context vector and is indifferent to where that vector came from. **AC-113** states exactly that scope, and is marked PROVISIONAL for the same reason.

### 4.2 Two pack axes, and the hard line

Packs are TOML, shallow, validated in Rust with spans, on two axes. One axis owns structure; the other owns bindings and contributions through named extension points — the resolution every build system that hit this tension converged on (Nx targets vs executors, Tekton Pipelines vs Tasks, Buildpacks' ordered detect-and-contribute, GitHub reusable workflows vs composite actions) [documented, proposal §4.2.1].

| Layer | Owns | Never owns | Enforced by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Process pack** | stages, activities, gates, rigor per classification; **roles** (`implementer`, `reviewer`, `investigator`, `spec-author`); tier policy per role; **slots** (named extension points in a stage) | any agent card; any toolchain command | ADR-0014 [documented] |
| **Agent card** | identity, heuristics, return format, capabilities; which role(s) it *fulfils*; an optional *selector* | orchestration; model choice — the tier lives on the role | AC-118 (a card setting `model` fails `doctor`); ADR-0014 |
| **Technology pack** | toolchain adapter config; **skills** attached by rule; **specialised cards** fulfilling a role under a selector; **slot fillers** inserted at declared slots | new stages; edits to process activities; rubric rows; `require` rows | AC-080, AC-081, AC-082; ADR-0014, ADR-0029 [documented] |

**The hard line: a technology pack may bind and contribute at slots; it may never restructure.** A `[stage.*]` table in a technology pack, a collision with a process pack's activity id, and a rubric row or acceptance requirement contributed by a technology pack are each refused at load as a configuration error naming the file and span — AC-080, AC-081, AC-082 respectively, all configuration-time, all zero-dispatch (AC-098). When an ecosystem genuinely needs a different shape — a cook/bake stage that is not a pre-test slot — that is a **process-pack variant** (`story-ue`) selected at triage or by config, and the run record names the variant and the source of that selection, both non-null (AC-085), so the shape change is attributable to one named choice rather than to two packs silently merging. AC-085 is honestly a **schema-presence** criterion: it proves the choice was recorded, never that it was right.

### 4.3 The binder — and equal specificity is a configuration error

The binder is deterministic Rust, run at plan time and recorded in the event log [documented, proposal §4.2.1; ADR-0014].

| Binder rule | Behaviour | Criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. Role → candidate cards → most specific selector wins | Two candidates at **equal specificity** for the item at hand are a **configuration error**, never a guess: resolution fails naming both cards, and the binder never orders them by file order, load order or name | **AC-087**; a property test that shuffling pack load order never turns an ambiguous case into a resolved one |
| 1b. No card matches a (role, toolchain) pair | `doctor` reports an unresolved role and exits non-zero, before any run is planned | **AC-086** |
| 1c. Mixed-toolchain item | Resolves through **toolchain-keyed artifact kinds**, one card per keyed kind; **no specificity tie-break is consulted for the mixed case** | **AC-088**; ADR-0073 (partially supersedes ADR-0029) |
| 2. Skills attach by rule (toolchain × role × stage × file-glob) and compile into the card's stable prefix | The skill set is therefore inside the card digest and inside the activity memo key: editing a skill invalidates the memo for every activity bound to that card | **AC-091**. F16 measured cache-read share (no-commit 1.000 · commit-each 0.689 · `--system-prompt` 1.000) [measured, F16, 50 dispatches]; the step from "a stable prefix pays once" to "the card must be in the digest" is [inferred], not measured |
| 3. Slot fillers insert in declaration order | A filler naming an undeclared slot is a configuration error; two packs filling one slot yield **both** fillers, in declaration order, with byte-identical resolution records | **AC-083**, **AC-084** |
| 4. The resolution is written to the run record | Card, matched selector, attached skills, inserted fillers, tier — five fields, so "why did this run with that card" is answerable from recorded fields alone | **AC-090**, falsifiable by running `redkiln explain` **with the pack directory renamed away** |

**AC-087 and AC-088 must not be one code path.** Specificity still ranks selectors for the single-toolchain case; the anti-`specificity_tiebreak` lint of AC-088 must be scoped to the mixed case or it breaks AC-087. ADR-0073's stated measured cause — a mixed Node+UE item silently receiving the UE change summary only, cited there as "F35 §1.3" — **is not in the evidence brief**: F35's ledger row records unsatisfiability, dead activities, ties, cycles and runtime, and has no such sub-finding. The rule stands on ADR-0073's authority as [documented] and is flagged evidence-thin; it must be re-grounded, and its most consequential instance (`ChangeSummary`) is in any case blocked on S-BIN.

Binder determinism is asserted **cross-machine** and instrumented as such: a same-process double-run cannot detect path-order, locale or hash-seed dependence, so **AC-089** is a two-OS CI matrix comparing resolution digests as build artifacts. AC-089 is determinism **given a fixed facet vector**; the stability of an LLM-emitted vector is the separate, blocked question of §4.1.

### 4.4 Selection is a pure function of four digests, in seven steps

`select()` is a function of `(pack digest, catalog digest, context digest, artifact digests)` and of nothing else [documented, catalog §2.5; ADR-0024].

| Step | What it does | The invariant that makes it safe | Criterion |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 · **Rigor** | Per stage from `policy.<class>` or an explicit item-frontmatter override (logged). `forbidden` is a **logged skip**, not an absence | Rigor is ordinal and admits comparison only; `forbidden` means nothing in that stage runs, **including via another activity's `requires`** | AC-050; AC-051 (load), **AC-077** (runtime non-dispatch) |
| 2 · **Required set** | `R(stage, ctx)` = `baseline` ∪ artifacts of every `require` row whose `when` holds | Every predicate resolves to artifact presence, a digest comparison, a **registered facet value** — an ordinal, a **set** membership (`toolchain` and `change` today, `surfaces` under ADR-0089), or a boolean's `true` / `false` / **`null`** — or a typed verdict; a judgement predicate ("stakeholders in agreement") fails load with `JudgementPredicate`. **A boolean facet with no evidence is `null`, never `false`** (ADR-0089): absent inputs are already `null` (ADR-0032) and `==`/`!=` against `null` is defined while ordering against it is a lint error (ADR-0069), so a `require` row whose predicate cannot be evaluated over a `null` yields `Unsatisfiable` and escalates to the human gate rather than resolving silently. Every `require` row over a boolean facet must now handle `null` explicitly, and one that does not is a lint error rather than a silent `false` | AC-065; ADR-0033, ADR-0050, **ADR-0089** |
| 3 · **Short-circuit** | Per artifact: `Present` / `Stale` / `Missing`, decided on `derived_from` digests | A missing `derived_from` entry is `Stale`, never assumed fresh. A **vouched root** (human-supplied, `derived_from = []`) stays `Present`; drift against it is an **advisory** that can never promote itself to a block | AC-066, AC-067; ADR-0025. `stale_policy` itself is **PROVISIONAL — P2 unrun** (AC-068) |
| 4 · **Producer** | Candidates = activities with the kind in `provides`, `applicable_if` true, `min_rigor ≤ stage rigor`, `stage` equal to the requiring stage; **lowest `method_rank` wins**; recurse on the producer's `requires` | **A tie is a pack validation error, never a runtime choice** (SHOP2 ordered methods). No candidate ⇒ `Unsatisfiable { kind, failed_predicates }` ⇒ gate | AC-046, **AC-047**, AC-052, **AC-054** |
| 5 · **Validate** | Acyclic; every `requires` bound; every element of `R` reachable; `Σ cost_class` within the item's appetite; `excludes` respected. Emits `dag_assembled { inputs_digest, nodes, edges, rows_fired, skipped }` | The engine **never trims, defers or drops** a member of `R` to fit the appetite — it gates, carrying the DAG, the appetite and the overage | AC-058, **AC-078** (runtime acyclicity), AC-059/AC-060 (load-time cycle and `excludes` symmetry) |
| 6 · **Execute** | Topological order; antichains parallel where §7 allows | The engine's own invariant is that execution **cannot change `R`** — step 7 is the only channel, and it re-enters at step 2 | **No criterion in the thirteen area files covers step 6.** §7.7 states four parallelism rules and names exactly one criterion (**AC-388**, the stagger); the sequential-within-a-tree, one-worktree-per-antichain-member and merge-queue rules are pinned by **AC-189** and **AC-196**–**AC-198** in `ac-git-reconcile.md` §1.7 (§7.7 below), and **the execute step of the pure selection function itself is uncovered**. Recorded as a gap, not papered over |
| 7 · **Deviation** | An activity may return `RequestDeviation { target, kind, claimed_predicate, why }`. The engine re-evaluates the predicate against evidence: true ⇒ re-run 2–5 and log the delta; false ⇒ gate with the request and its evaluated operands attached | **The agent never edits `R`.** `RequiredSet` is constructible only inside the selection crate and implements no deserialisation, so no agent-returned value can reach it; a `kind` outside the activity's own `deviation_ok` is refused as a configuration error and never reaches predicate evaluation | AC-061, AC-062, AC-063, AC-064; ADR-0046 |

**Lowest rank wins; a tie is a validation error.** This is the one place the design refuses a defensible-looking fallback. AC-047 fails load with `RankTie { kind, context, activities }` before any activity is dispatched, and the shipped catalog's tie count must stay 0 as a golden assertion. Measured baseline: **rank ties 0** across the enumerated lattice [measured, F35, n=1 enumeration, one machine]. The stronger claim — that the engine exposes *no* runtime tie-break at all, no code path, no declaration-order fallback — was **cut from AC-047** as an absence claim over a crate that no named instrument decides; it survives as design intent in ADR-0056 only. Note also that once ties and cycles are build failures they cannot recur without CI failing, so those two zeroes carry no discriminating content (SM-014's own honesty note).

**Unsatisfiable produces a typed gate and nothing else.** No next-best rank, no relaxation of `min_rigor`, no substitute kind, no dropping the artifact from `R`; the producer-resolution function has no default arm (AC-054, a negative criterion). The gate record names the kind, every failed predicate, **the evaluated value of each facet that predicate read — which may itself be `null`, recorded as an abstention with the rule that produced it and never coerced to `false`** (ADR-0089; the route is AC-079's existing `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`, extended to the boolean facets, and ADR-0067's `recent_deploy` under `R-recent-deploy-feed-absent` is its live precedent) — and the rigor in force, and the store's single write path refuses a record missing any of them (AC-055) — the shape F31 showed typed payloads actually get filled (schema conformance **52/52 = 100%**, `repro.command` **147/147**) [measured, F31].

**The reproducibility contract.** Any run that cannot be replayed to the same DAG has a **hidden input**, and the memo key is then **unsound** [documented, catalog §2.5; ADR-0024]. This is enforced in three places, not asserted:

- **AC-041** — the selection crate cannot read wall-clock time, environment variables, the filesystem, the network or a random source; such a call **fails to compile** rather than reading at runtime (`trybuild` compile-fail plus a dependency/import lint). The precedent is measured: a default `claude -p` silently inherited 12 MCP servers and ~280 tools, **56% of the cache write**, and an ignored isolation flag looks identical to an honoured one [measured, F8].
- **AC-042** — `redkiln replay --run <id>` re-derives the DAG from the logged inputs alone and reports `HiddenInput { run_id, first_divergent_node }` on any mismatch, never a warning. **This is a first measurement.** F19's stated limit is that **the FSM was never replayed** — 174/174 suspensions were lossless, but "same next action" is unproven [measured, F19, one operator]. SM-013 records **no baseline** and reports non-identical runs as a count, never as a percentage rounded to 100%.
- **AC-043** — a memo entry minted by a run whose replay assertion failed is refused and re-materialised (`memo_evicted { reason: unsound_replay }`); one served hit from a non-replayable run is a correctness defect, not a rate to manage (SM-020). The memo key admits no construction that omits any of the four digests (AC-045).

Selection spends **$0.00** in model dispatches, always — the selection crate does not contain the `Harness` port or any adapter that can dispatch a model (AC-044), cross-checked at runtime by SM-019. Any model spend attributed to selection falsifies purity and invalidates every replay claim in this area.

### 4.5 The catalog v0

| Element | v0 shape | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Activities | **102** in the lattice corpus over **12** packs; **90** authored in the P6 pass, against a curated **~95**-activity v0 ceiling with the remaining raw rows in an **unshipped extended file** | 102 / 12 / 90 are [measured, F35/F34]. **The ~95 ceiling is not measured**: ADR-0027's own consequences section records ~95/~170 as its approximate split of the 267 raw sourced activities, with no spike or count behind it — [documented, ADR-0027], planning estimate |
| Stages | Twelve, from `triage` (always run) to `closeout` (`finally`); kind-level constraints encoded as **`forbidden` rows**, not absence — incidents forbid discovery/spec/design, spikes forbid release | [documented, catalog §4] |
| Step kinds | **Five, closed**: `agent` (the only place a model runs), `tool` (a toolchain verb through the port), `check` (a deterministic predicate in Rust; cannot dispatch), `gate` (the kernel writes the mark and **exits 0**, resuming into the same step), `emit` (binds outputs to `provides`; exactly one per kind provided; terminal) | [documented, activity-graph schema §1]; ADR-0022 |
| Typed exits | **Three**: `Materialized { artifacts, evidence }`, `Blocked { reason, question }`, `RequestDeviation { … }`. A step failure with retries exhausted is `Blocked`, not an error the stage interprets. Nothing else leaks. **Alternative outcomes** (a canary that rolls back still "completes") are expressed by a final materialising step rather than a fourth exit — ADR-0041, **fully superseded by ADR-0064**, and the ADR README warns that anyone acting on 0041 must read 0064's Decision section for what survives; both ids are named here so the narrowing is reachable | [documented, schema §3]; ADR-0036 (evidence minting is closed per step kind), **ADR-0041 → ADR-0064** |
| Activity lifecycle | A coherence edit to a **shipped** activity bumps its `schema_version` and enters the changelog — a shipped activity is never edited in place without both (**AC-111**, **AC-117**) | ADR-0072 [documented] |
| Registries | **Five**, one table per value with a date and a type: `artifacts.toml` (kinds, `source`, `keyed_by`), `facets.toml` (normative for the `toolchain` and `change` facets; under **ADR-0089** its `source` field gains a companion **`deriver`** naming the instrument, and it carries the set-valued `surfaces` facet whose values come from a checked-in `surfaces.toml` path-glob map), `registry.toml` (toolchain verbs and feeds; a verb's `live = true` implies `memo = false` on every step using it), `questions.toml` (question ids, closed option sets, `attaches`), and the checklist registry | ADR-0057, ADR-0059, ADR-0061, ADR-0038/ADR-0063, ADR-0040 [documented] |

**Authoring is tractable, and the kill line was never approached.** Three authoring passes — **by one operator, not three independent people** — landed at median **56.5** (F32), **73** (F33, +29%), and **53** non-blank lines with sum 5,059 over 90 activities (F34), against a >150 kill: the shipped corpus clears by ~**2.8×** [measured, F32/F33/F34, n=3 passes, one operator]. First-run lint errors per file ran **4.7 → 1.3 → 0.11**, but the P6 author **read the lint before authoring**, which F34 states explicitly, so the 43× improvement is not attributable to the lint (SM-030). P9's prediction that amendments would fall was **falsified at n=3** (F33), so SM-034 is a **TRACKER with no target and no kill** and must not be cited downstream as assurance that the schema is stabilising. The lint's own precision is **13 real / 34 false = 27.7%** on one pass and it **accepted 5 guesses silently** across 27 ambiguities on another [measured, F32/F33] — so **AC-107** makes silent resolution impossible (`Err(Ambiguous)`, never a most-likely target), and SM-029 starts *in* its own kill state at 27.7% against a ~30% line, which is stated so nobody reads it as a regression.

**Where the schema amendments live, and which are in scope here.** ADR-0031–ADR-0073 are cited as a range in §0.1 and §1.1, and **twenty of them are SCHEMA-series amendments that this document does not reflect individually**. That is deliberate: they fix the *activity-graph schema's grammar*, whose home is `references/2026-09-07-activity-graph-schema.md`, and a design document that restated them would be duplicating a spec it does not own. They are named here in groups so a reader can find them rather than discover them by diffing:

- **Ref and predicate grammar** — absent optional inputs are `null` and quantifiers over an empty set are vacuously true (**ADR-0032**); a coalesce operator in refs (**ADR-0034**); parentheses and quoted facet-value literals in predicates (**ADR-0048**); quantifier bodies admit full comparisons (**ADR-0049**), and `len()` plus nesting (**ADR-0070**); ordering operators against `null` are lint errors while equality is defined (**ADR-0069**); `rigor` is a legal root in `applicable_if` (**ADR-0066**).
- **Structure and loops** — `for_each` sources and fan-out output projection are fixed syntactic forms (**ADR-0035**); `on_false` is a bounded table and never a bare scalar (**ADR-0044**); self-reference inside a loop binds the **previous** iteration (**ADR-0055**); a `gate` step inside a `for_each` is a lint error (**ADR-0068**); `After=<step>` shorthand is **deferred** (**ADR-0039**).
- **Tags, facets and artifacts** — same facet disjoins, distinct facets conjoin (**ADR-0050**); provided artifacts are unconditional at or above `min_rigor` (**ADR-0051**); optional artifact refs are legal and alternation inside `requires` is not (**ADR-0054**); boolean facets are `applicable_if`-only and tagging one is a lint error (**ADR-0060**, whose criterion **AC-109** is uncited by any section and is owed a home).
- **Lint rules** — rule 3 resolves paths against the step's **output** schema (**ADR-0052**); **R4b** requires an `on_false` target to be reachable at every rigor at which the check is active (**ADR-0065**).
- **Evidence and lifecycle** — empty evidence is legal **only** for activities with no agent step (**ADR-0071**); and **ADR-0072**, the one of the twenty that is *not* schema grammar, is reflected above as the shipped-activity version-and-changelog rule.

**Five registry facts constrain what may feed selection, today — three of registration and two of measurement.** `binary_change` is **measured but unregistered in `facets.toml`**, so it can feed no `require` row; `dynamism` is **pre-registered with no registry entry**; `recent_deploy` is **underivable at this pin**, notwithstanding ADR-0067 admitting it as a derived boolean [documented, brief §E contested list]. **AC-072** and **AC-108** make all three fail load if named in any `applicable_if`, `require.when` or `claimed_predicate` — an unregistered or underivable facet cannot silently become a selection input. **F39 adds the other two, and they are about the values rather than the entries.** First, `binary_change` is now also **measured wrong**: precision **0.60** [0.23, 0.88] at recall 1.00 over five emitted positives, so registering it would not on its own make it usable. Second — and this is the larger of the two — the facets that *are* registered and *are* derived are the ones carrying no signal: **`auth` scores 0.00 [0.00, 0.26] over eleven positives, every one false, and `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66]**, while `data_migration` emits **no positive at all** on this corpus and is **not measurable** rather than passing or failing [measured, F39; 140 cells at pin `47faec9f`, $0, panel-adjudicated]. **ADR-0089** is the record: a facet may not declare `source = "derived"` without a named `deriver` and a scored precision record for it; a lexical scan of free text does not satisfy PC8 rule 2 for `require`-row purposes; the repo-wide `auth` boolean is withdrawn for a set-valued `surfaces` facet over a checked-in `surfaces.toml`, **unscored until S-FACET runs**; and a derived boolean that cannot be computed is `null`, routed to AC-079's gate. The registry loader must recognise the set shape explicitly — `surfaces` is a third registry shape alongside boolean and ordinal, which ADR-0073 keeps in separate registries.

### 4.6 Completeness is CI, not a per-file lint

| Lattice reading | Pre-registered | 12 packs | After decisions 1–10 | Pre-registered kill |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Unsatisfiable `(stage, ctx)` pairs | **23.5%** | **11.4%** | **0.3%** | >5% — **fires twice**, clears post-decision |
| Dead activities | **20.8%** (8 packs) | **14.9%** = 15/101 | **5.9%** = **6/102** | >20% — fires by **0.8 points** at 8 packs, clears at 12 |
| Rank ties | — | — | **0** | 0 tolerated |
| Hard cycles | — | — | **0** | 0 tolerated |
| Lattice runtime | — | — | **27 s** | — |

[measured, F35, n=1 enumeration, one machine, one catalog]. **Both readings are reported**: the pre-registered reading fires the kill twice and the amended lattice clears all three criteria. Two consequences follow and are carried as criteria.

**First: a green per-file lint is never evidence of catalog completeness (AC-100).** The unsatisfiable cells arise from interaction between activities in **different files**, so CI runs the whole-catalog lattice independently of the per-file lint, with a regression fixture that is individually clean in every file and still fails the build. The area brief records that **three quarters of first-run gaps were invisible to a per-file lint** and were found by the **27-second** whole-catalog run [documented, area brief — the F35 ledger row carries the aggregate rates, **not that split**, so the fraction may not be quoted as measured; the 27 s is measured]. AC-101 fails the build above the >5% line, AC-102 above the >20% dead line, AC-103 on any tie or cycle at zero tolerance, and AC-104/AC-116 enforce family totality and cap rank escalation at the highest total rigor — **on ADR-0058's authority**, which declares `family` a catalog field and makes sharing and totality lint-checkable; ADR-0056 supplies the escalation cap, not the totality check, and this document previously credited it with both.

**Second: a rate reported without its pack set is not a reading.** 8 packs versus 12 moved the dead-activity figure **5.9 points with the catalog unchanged** [measured, F35], so SM-027 reports the pack count alongside every reading and forbids comparing two readings taken at different pack counts. **The pack count is itself unaccepted.** The 12 are 8 pre-registered plus **four supplementary packs — `chore`, `tech-debt`, `security-vuln`, `docs` — which METHOD-P §3.2 has neither accepted nor struck** (F35 §3.1; HANDOFF "what to do next" item 3). Every 12-pack figure in this document — the 0.3% unsatisfiable and the 6/102 dead reading included — therefore rests on a pack set that is **one third provisional**, and §0.1's DECIDED catalog row is decided on the *semantics*, not on that pack set. Discharge: **P6-PACKS** (§10.4/§10.5), one decision per supplementary pack, plus the v0 pack tagging step DECISIONS-2026-09-09 still lists as open work item (1); AC-057 writes both numbers into the lint report artifact keyed by catalog version. The two rate measures are, for now, **tripwires and not live signals**: 0.3% sits **16× inside** its 5% line (SM-026), and the verbosity median has never been at risk in three passes (SM-031). A green reading on either is not evidence that anything works.

**Cost of the check.** The whole-catalog lattice cost **$0** in model dispatches (P6, P9, P9b and the lattice work all did) and ran in **27 s** [measured, F35 / brief §D]. SM-025 records that the brief **does not carry the pack count for that 27 s run**, and that how the lattice scales at 2× catalog size is **untested** — a hypothesis the measure exists to check, not a target.

### 4.7 Rigor is an ordinal deontic enum — and the ladder is not yet monotone

Rigor is `forbidden < discouraged < optional < recommended < mandatory` (ADR-0020, ADR-0023 — the same decision recorded in two homes, `related`, neither withdrawing from the other). It is **ordinal and never summed**: `Rigor` implements no `Add`, `Sum` or `Into<f64>`, and code that tries to combine rigor values arithmetically does not compile (AC-050, a `trybuild` compile-fail test). **The closed-policy risk is named where the decision is taken, not only where it is falsified.** A small enum that could not compose is a documented, dated, first-party failure shape: the Dagster RFC records exactly that outcome and its replacement by composable primitives [documented, 1P Dagster RFC on closed enums; research pass §4 rec 22]. The ordinal enum is adopted knowing that risk, and §10.6 registers "the catalog lint cannot be made monotone" as the result that would realise it. The justification for choosing an ordinal over a weighted score is external and general: **"rank reversal of one or another type occurs for virtually every MCDM method"**, TOPSIS and Simple Additive Weighting included, because weighted sums ignore that normalisation scale units differ across criteria — cite the general result, not an AHP-specific one, so "just use TOPSIS" is pre-answered [peer, ScienceDirect S0895717711007850, fetched 2026-09-08]. The local corroboration is that the judgement input to such a rubric is itself unstable: **verdict flaky on 2 of 4 items** over repeated draws while detection was flaky on **0 of 7** over 21 draws [measured, F27, n=2–3].

**The measured defect: the ladder is not monotone in internal quality.** Escalating to `mandatory` with `has_repro` currently selects `bisect`, which has **no adversary step and no policy step** — a higher rigor level that *drops* quality apparatus a lower one carried [measured mechanism, F37 / brief C.10]. This is cited as a **mechanism and never as a rate**: F37's primary criterion was **powerless at n=3**, every Wilson interval overlapping by construction, so no rate may be read off it.

The fix is a new lint rule, not a catalog patch:

| Rule | Statement | Instrument |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Rank monotonicity** (ADR-0081; **AC-105**) | For any facet vector and any adjacent rigor levels *r* and *r+1*, the check fails if the graph selected at *r+1* omits a quality-apparatus step — adversary, policy, or a declared quality block — that the graph selected at *r* contains | A lattice CI monotonicity check across the **full facet × rigor cross-product**, plus a regression fixture reproducing the measured defect |

SM-035 records the baseline honestly: **at least one violation exists today**, the cross-product has **not** been enumerated, so the count is a **lower bound of 1, not a rate**; the target is 0, enforced as a build failure; and if violations cannot be removed without collapsing the ladder, then rigor is not a single ordered axis and the deontic enum needs a second dimension.

**Two things this area explicitly does not do.** It does not set the **quality shorthand default** — that is BLOCKED (ADR-0080; P7 powerless at n=3, F37), AC-105 constrains the ladder's *shape* and never its default level, and the forbidden predicate `findings.none(evidence in [Reproduced, MutationSurvived])` may not be used as a terminal condition. And it quotes **no rigor-escalation cost multiplier**: F37's mandatory-arm ratio is reported as **3.66×** in F37 and **4.4×** in the decisions log, unreconciled, and the mandatory arm was dropped at n=1 (packs-catalog gap 7). What *is* citable is a cost and a constant predicate: `adversary → policy` cost **2.64×** ($2.69 vs $1.02 per run), and its policy check was **false on 33 of 33** [measured, F37, 61 runs, 297 records, 0 void, $131.62]. **"Changed the root cause on 1 item in 10" is not citable and is excluded from that set**: it is one cell (`issue-81`, **0/3 against 1/3**) moved by a single draw of three — the powerless criterion's own output, not a result — and F37 §2 states that "nothing here excludes 0 and nothing excludes considerably more".

### 4.8 Decisions

| # | Decision | Alternatives rejected | Why | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 4-A | Two pack axes with a hard line: process packs own structure, technology packs bind and contribute at slots only | One pack type with an override algebra; technology packs allowed to append stages | Two packs silently merging makes the shape unattributable; the escape hatch is a named process-pack **variant** recorded in the run record | ADR-0014, ADR-0029 [documented]; AC-080..AC-085 |
| 4-B | Binder resolves role → card by most specific selector; **equal specificity is a configuration error** | Declaration order; file order; alphabetical; "most recently loaded wins" | Any implicit tiebreak makes the resolution a function of the filesystem, which AC-089's two-OS matrix would then fail | ADR-0014 [documented]; AC-087 |
| 4-C | Mixed-toolchain items resolve through **toolchain-keyed artifact kinds**, one instance per toolchain — the specificity tiebreak is withdrawn for the five keyed kinds | Keep "toolchain beats generic" for mixed items | The withdrawn rule silently delivered one toolchain's artifact for a two-toolchain item. **Evidence-thin**: ADR-0073's cited cause ("F35 §1.3") is not in the brief; the rule stands on ADR authority and `ChangeSummary`'s type is blocked on S-BIN | ADR-0073 (partial supersession of ADR-0029) [documented]; AC-088, AC-049 |
| 4-D | `select()` is a **pure function of four digests**, seven steps, with purity enforced at compile time and replay asserted per run | A "practical" purity by convention; reading the clock or the filesystem for convenience and documenting it | A hidden input makes the memo key unsound; F8 measured ambient environment entering a dispatch invisibly | ADR-0024 [documented]; AC-040..AC-045; F8, F19 [measured] |
| 4-E | Lowest `method_rank` wins; **a tie is a validation error at load**, never a runtime choice | Runtime tie-break by specificity, declaration order or cost | SHOP2's ordered-methods discipline; measured ties are 0, so the rule costs nothing today and forbids a whole class of silent divergence tomorrow | ADR-0056 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-047 |
| 4-F | Unsatisfiable ⇒ **typed gate**, with no fallback of any kind; over-appetite ⇒ gate, never a trimmed `R` | Next-best rank; relax `min_rigor`; substitute kind; drop the artifact | A silently reduced required set is the failure this whole model exists to prevent; the 0.3% residual must surface, not be papered over | ADR-0024, ADR-0056 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-054, AC-055, AC-058 |
| 4-G | Deviation is typed, per-activity, and **never edits `R`**; `RequiredSet` has no `Deserialize` impl | Let a warranted deviation write the required set directly; drop the channel as unused | Deviation was **offered 71 times and used 0** in 61 runs, so the channel must be closed by construction rather than by disuse — and the warranted fraction has an **empty denominator**, not a 0% | ADR-0046 [documented]; F37 [measured]; AC-061..AC-064, SM-017 |
| 4-H | Catalog completeness is **whole-catalog CI**, not a per-file lint; readings always carry their pack count | Per-file lint only; nightly-only lattice | Gaps arise across files; the check costs 27 s and $0, and the same rate moves 5.9 points on pack set alone | ADR-0027 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-100..AC-104, SM-027 |
| 4-I | Rigor is an **ordinal deontic enum**, never summed, and the ladder must be **monotone in internal quality** (new lint rule) | Weighted numeric stage weights (the proposal's 0.2/0.5/1.0); any MCDM weighted sum | Rank reversal is endemic to weighted MCDM methods; and the measured ladder already drops the quality block at `mandatory` | ADR-0020, ADR-0023, **ADR-0058** (families as a declared, lint-checked catalog field — the record behind the AC-104/AC-116 totality check), ADR-0081; ScienceDirect S0895717711007850 [peer]; **Dagster RFC on closed enums** [documented, 1P] — the closed-policy risk this decision accepts; F37 [measured mechanism] |
| 4-J | **No decision is taken on the LLM-emitted context-vector source.** ADR-0079 stays `proposed`; every ordinal axis is `source = "human"` until AC-071's gate passes | Amend ADR-0079 into a decision; raise or lower temperature; rewrite anchors again; ship the alpha<0.6 rule as the predicate; **read F39 as deciding 0079** | The kill fired 4/4; the anchor-rewrite repair already failed at full n; the alpha rule is not decidable on this corpus; T_bg = 0 is measured for a Claude model, so decoding noise is not the mechanism. **F39 scores arm D and discharges AC-071's fourth clause and no other** — it measures a *derived* vector where 0079 asks about an *LLM-emitted* one, so it narrows the options without deciding among them; the block now stands on clause 1, identity **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against 0.80 | ADR-0079 [documented]; F36, **F39** [measured]; arXiv 2603.04417 / 2604.22411 / 2511.01176 [preprint] |
| 4-K | **A decision *is* taken on the derived source, and it is not the same question.** A facet's computed fact must name its evidence source and provenance class (**DECLARED > DERIVED > PROPOSED > ASKED**); `facets.toml`'s `source` gains a companion **`deriver`** field and no facet may declare `source = "derived"` without a scored record for that deriver; the repo-wide `auth` boolean is **withdrawn** for a set-valued `surfaces` facet over a checked-in `surfaces.toml`; and **boolean facets become tri-valued, absence of evidence `null` and routed to AC-079's `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`** | Anchor the regex and re-score it (retained as **S-FACET arm 2**, a control, never the answer); add keywords; put a confidence score on the rule; buy CodeQL, Semgrep or a call graph; train a classifier on this corpus; do nothing until S-FACET reports | `auth` at **0.00 over eleven positives** with an upper bound of 0.26 carries **no signal**, and anchoring removes the *coauthor* (8) and ARIA-`role` (2) hits while leaving *authored* (40) and design-`token` (36) untouched — a finding about the method, not a tuning parameter. Four empty-diff items produced **sixteen confident booleans** the panel would not answer. And "do nothing" was rejected because AC-071 gates `source = "llm"` only, so the registry stays **ungated while measurably wrong**; the abstention half is small, buildable, and independent of which deriver eventually wins | **ADR-0089** (partial supersession of ADR-0030, which stays `accepted`) [documented]; **F39** [measured]; `references/2026-09-09-change-classification.md` §2, §4, §6a [documented] |
